sakuya_b6_player_in_power_bloc_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes is_in_power_bloc = yes }
}

sakuya_b6_has_marked_power_bloc_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target } }
}

sakuya_b6_has_marked_country_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target } }
}

sakuya_b6_has_marked_country_in_power_bloc_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target is_in_power_bloc = yes } }
}

sakuya_b6_marked_country_in_marked_power_bloc_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        any_country = {
            has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
            is_in_power_bloc = yes
            any_country = {
                has_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target
                is_in_power_bloc = yes
                power_bloc = prev.power_bloc
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b6_mark_player_power_bloc_selected_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_in_power_bloc = yes
        any_country = {
            has_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target
            is_in_power_bloc = yes
            root = { power_bloc = prev.power_bloc }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b6_mark_target_country_power_bloc_selected_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        any_country = {
            has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
            is_in_power_bloc = yes
            any_country = {
                has_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target
                is_in_power_bloc = yes
                power_bloc = prev.power_bloc
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b6_mark_player_power_bloc_effect = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        sakuya_b6_clear_all_marked_power_bloc_effect = yes
        if = {
            limit = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
            power_bloc = {
                power_bloc_leader = {
                    set_variable = { name = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target value = 1 }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b6_mark_target_country_power_bloc_effect = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        sakuya_b6_clear_all_marked_power_bloc_effect = yes
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target is_in_power_bloc = yes }
            power_bloc = {
                power_bloc_leader = {
                    set_variable = { name = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target value = 1 }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b6_clear_marked_power_bloc_effect = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = { sakuya_b6_clear_all_marked_power_bloc_effect = yes }
}

sakuya_b6_fx_set_cohesion_100 = {

# ============================================================
# B6 TAB1 - STATE

sakuya_b6_has_home_marked_state_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
}

sakuya_b6_has_foreign_marked_state_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target } }
}

sakuya_b6_has_any_marked_state_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
		}
	}
}
